# P4 — дополнительный nit, editorial/style debt and closed calibrations

Навигация: [P0](P0.md) · [P1](P1.md) · [P2](P2.md) · [P3](P3.md) · [P4](P4.md) · [primary coverage](#supplemental-primary-coverage) · [plans](#supplemental-plan-index).

## Правило среза и ограничения

Дата дополнительной консолидации: 2026-10-09; входной independent cycle-2 датирован 2026-10-08.

**Это дополнительный risk/debt срез, НЕ исходный plan priority и НЕ новая независимая очередь.** Пользователь отдельно назначил P3 для scoped low и P4 для nit/style. Во всех 189 полных входных reports исходные plan priorities — P0/P1/P2; источник не объявляет исходных P3/P4. Lowercase percentile p99/p99.9 не превращён в P-уровень. Исходные P0.md/P1.md/P2.md сохранены без изменений: их 3176 primary decisions, 447 plans (91/194/162) и 34 source-verdict пересмотра не переразносятся.

Основа — последняя принятая статическая консолидация [P0](P0.md#методика-и-проверенный-срез), [P1](P1.md#методика-и-проверенный-срез), [P2](P2.md#методика-и-проверенный-срез), а не исходные cycle-2 verdicts как готовое доказательство. Все 189 входных Markdown и их history вновь доступны целиком в памяти: 84777 строк / 7740601 Unicode characters. Текущие IDs/risk взяты только из authoritative prefixes; collapsed history не создаёт дополнительных строк. Пояснения/callers/oracles/registration и exact dependency qualifications переиспользованы из accepted canon; для новых facets отдельно названа граница. 801 обнаруженных cited source/contract/config текстов прочитаны без исполнения и fingerprinted при этом продолжении; это не заявление о повторном exhaustive source audit.

Frozen e3765c93 — происхождение входных reports, не сегодняшний working-tree guarantee. Current file:line здесь относится к прочитанному изменённому дереву; внешние пользовательские source/toolchain/dependency правки не принадлежат xxs. Pins/installed-target/runtime ограничения остаются в [exact source discipline](P0.md#exact-dependencycontract-дисциплина). Указанные Wasmtime 48.0.5 / rustls 0.23.45 / declared Rust 1.95.0 — прочитанные версии, не запущенные инструменты. Последовательные normalized UTF-8/LF fingerprints и readback не создают атомарного/deployed snapshot; непросмотренные installed/deployment facets не сертифицируются.

**Read-only:** в этом продолжении записываются только P3.md и P4.md. Ни compiler/cargo/build/lint/fmt/tests/benchmarks, ни проект/воспроизведения, ни новые агенты не запускались; auxiliary/temp файлов и git mutation нет. Наличие теста, registry/manifest и Cargo.lock означает соответствующий source fact/provenance, не выполненную проверку или callable oracle seam. Стоимости структурные, без количественных измерений. Нет generic quota/validation recipes, придуманных consumer seams или новых future oracles.

Verdict source row в ledger всегда принадлежит accepted P0–P2. Supplemental facet verdict описывает именно выбранный остаток: fixed — конкретный source-fixed mechanism, refuted — named counter-evidence, N/A — policy/optional redesign/calibration, unverified — missing premise, не safe. Closed/higher-risk redirects исключены из active table. Mixed bundle не понижен целиком: original risk и P allocation видны рядом с link на существующий canon. Составной row может покрывать несколько leaves; его primary count всё равно один.

## Арифметика и пересечение с P0–P2

Полный authoritative risk census: low **882**, nit **445**, medium **555**, high **159**, — **688** = **2729 Claim+Observation** (2635+94); ещё 447 Plan в этот risk знаменатель не входят. Raw low/nit — duplicate-inclusive строки, не bugs. После qualification supplemental primary union = **1455**: 882 raw-low + 445 raw-nit + 2 accepted-low SDK-doc rows с исходной medium меткой + 126 отдельных style/calibration rows с исходной — меткой. Каждый selected source ID primary ровно в одном из P3/P4. Все 28 raw lower-risk observations присутствуют: 27 имеют primary P3 и 1 — primary P4.

**Totals между original и supplemental views НЕ суммируются.** Эти source IDs уже входят в 3176 решений P0–P2; новые файлы показывают другой срез тех же данных. Канонические детали, plans и cross-references могут повторяться. Even P3/P4 facet counts не являются количеством независимых runtime bugs: сюда входят scope facets, maintenance bundles, закрытые calibration и redirects.

Этот файл: primary **532** = Claim 531 + Observation 1; no primary Plan rows. Selected facets/decision cards **241** = active/partial/unverified 172 + fixed/refuted/N/A 67 + higher-risk-only redirects 2. Related original plan references **105**; они не прибавляют bugs.

Risk qualification: все 882 raw-low остаются primary в P3, включая 17 rows, которые лишь redirect к четырём medium mechanisms. Из 445 raw-nit **39** имеют primary P3: 11 относятся к уже accepted-low boundaries, ещё 28 имеют конкретную conditional semantic/error/security/consumer boundary вместо чистого style. Оставшиеся **406** raw-nit primary в P4; четыре из них redirect к medium NEON/WS механизму, не active nits. Две SDK get-doc rows добавлены в P3 по уже accepted scoped-low doc canon, не вопреки его medium raw allegation. P4 дополнительно показывает 126 pure-style/structural calibrations: 53 текущие thematic style — rows, 54 same-guarantee linked counterparts и 19 явно проверенных summary/non-theme counterparts. Pure optional file splitting, visibility/root-export uniformity и wording choice не стали mandatory defect.

TASK 17 — отдельная qualification: accepted partial campaign status сохранён, tags source-fixed, но непоставленные legacy shims — N/A в этом дополнительном debt срезе при явно отсутствующем alpha upgrade promise. TASK 30 — structural source fixes не реактивированы; оставлена отдельная partial isolated-delivery/ignore-revs attribution facet. Runtime/error-return portions старых nit bundles cross-reference P3/существующие canonical guarantees, а не исчезают под стилевым названием.

### L4-RV-BENCH-UTILS-4E7A9919 — Reproducibility key described inconsistently: "(k, σ, seed) triple" vs. five values

**Дополнительный verdict:** `confirmed-open`. **Модули:** `shamir-bench-utils`. **Accepted canon:** [RV-BENCH-UTILS-4E7A9919](P1.md#rv-bench-utils-4e7a9919) (allocation P1, scoped risk `nit`, verdict `confirmed-open`).

**Механизм / обязанность / граница:** The module's three-value instruction omits n and dim. Function documentation specifies all five, and the consumer calls a five-value tuple a triple. Current report code prints the full inputs, so published omission is not established.

**Counter-scope / оставшаяся premise:** The module names only k, sigma, and seed, but n and dim affect output.

**Current source evidence:** [crates/shamir-bench-utils/src/vector_data.rs:29](../../../../crates/shamir-bench-utils/src/vector_data.rs); [crates/shamir-bench-utils/src/vector_data.rs:158](../../../../crates/shamir-bench-utils/src/vector_data.rs); [crates/shamir-engine/benches/vector_search.rs:19](../../../../crates/shamir-engine/benches/vector_search.rs); [crates/shamir-engine/examples/vector_report.rs:336](../../../../crates/shamir-engine/examples/vector_report.rs).

<a id="l4-rv-bench-utils-88d1646b"></a>

### L4-RV-BENCH-UTILS-88D1646B — Cargo.toml `description` advertises the removed BENCH_QUICK tier feature

**Дополнительный verdict:** `confirmed-open`. **Модули:** `shamir-bench-utils`. **Accepted canon:** [RV-BENCH-UTILS-88D1646B](P2.md#rv-bench-utils-88d1646b) (allocation P2, scoped risk `nit`, verdict `confirmed-open`).

**Механизм / обязанность / граница:** The package metadata still advertises tier functionality absent from the declaration-only current public surface. This is documentation drift, not an active environment-processing defect.

**Counter-scope / оставшаяся premise:** The package description names removed behavior; current exports are only vector_data and feature-gated peak_mem.

**Current source evidence:** [crates/shamir-bench-utils/Cargo.toml:6](../../../../crates/shamir-bench-utils/Cargo.toml); [crates/shamir-bench-utils/src/lib.rs:9](../../../../crates/shamir-bench-utils/src/lib.rs); [crates/shamir-bench-utils/src/lib.rs:17](../../../../crates/shamir-bench-utils/src/lib.rs).

<a id="l4-rv-bench-utils-f53f775f"></a>

### L4-RV-BENCH-UTILS-F53F775F — `round_robin_balances_clusters` asserts a statistical property as an exact equality

**Дополнительный verdict:** `unverified`. **Модули:** `shamir-bench-utils`. **Accepted canon:** [RV-BENCH-UTILS-F53F775F](P2.md#rv-bench-utils-f53f775f) (allocation P2, scoped risk `nit`, verdict `unverified`).

**Механизм / обязанность / граница:** Nearest-centroid counts are not a direct construction-label oracle; equal totals can even hide exchanged assignments. Nonetheless this deterministic seed may be well separated. No near-tie analysis or affected target proves a flake, so replacing equality with arbitrary tolerance is unjustified.

**Counter-scope / оставшаяся premise:** The oracle observes geometric assignment rather than i%k, and equal counts do not prove each point retained its construction label.

<a id="l4-rv-collections-201d84d3-facet"></a>

### L4-RV-COLLECTIONS-201D84D3-FACET — Public rustdoc discoverability and local lint-comment presentation

**Дополнительный verdict:** `confirmed-open`. **Модули:** `shamir-collections`. **Accepted canon:** [RV-COLLECTIONS-201D84D3](P1.md#rv-collections-201d84d3) (allocation P1, scoped risk `low`, verdict `confirmed-open`).

**Механизм / обязанность / граница:** THasher/eight constructors lack rustdoc and the local allow has no adjacent explanation. This nit facet concerns discoverability, not a new hasher or serialization requirement.

**Counter-scope / оставшаяся premise:** External sanction exists in clippy.toml, and doctest disabling has a rationale. The sanctioned suppression/optional containment choice has its own N/A calibration, not a mandatory runtime remediation.

**Current source evidence:** [crates/shamir-collections/src/lib.rs:17](../../../../crates/shamir-collections/src/lib.rs); [crates/shamir-collections/src/lib.rs:25](../../../../crates/shamir-collections/src/lib.rs); [crates/shamir-types/Cargo.toml:75](../../../../crates/shamir-types/Cargo.toml); [crates/shamir-collections/src/lib.rs:9](../../../../crates/shamir-collections/src/lib.rs); [crates/shamir-collections/Cargo.toml:16](../../../../crates/shamir-collections/Cargo.toml); [crates/shamir-query-types/Cargo.toml:52](../../../../crates/shamir-query-types/Cargo.toml); [clippy.toml:39](../../../../clippy.toml).

<a id="l4-rv-connect-349809b3"></a>

### L4-RV-CONNECT-349809B3 — Username normalization documentation promises sharp-s folding contradicted by pinned dependency and registered oracle

**Дополнительный verdict:** `confirmed-open`. **Модули:** `shamir-connect`. **Accepted canon:** [RV-CONNECT-349809B3](P2.md#rv-connect-349809b3) (allocation P2, scoped risk `nit`, verdict `confirmed-open`).

**Механизм / обязанность / граница:** username.rs says German sharp-s maps to ss and rejects describing its case rule as lowercase mapping. Exact precis-profiles 0.1.13 preserves already-lowercase sharp-s and uses char::to_lowercase when required, then NFC; published source https://docs.rs/crate/precis-profiles/0.1.13/source/src/common.rs. The registered German test positively expects straße, not strasse. Correct the documentation rather than changing established username identity semantics to fit its false example.

**Current source evidence:** [crates/shamir-connect/src/common/username.rs:8](../../../../crates/shamir-connect/src/common/username.rs); [crates/shamir-connect/src/common/username.rs:42](../../../../crates/shamir-connect/src/common/username.rs); [crates/shamir-connect/tests/integration_username_precis.rs:35](../../../../crates/shamir-connect/tests/integration_username_precis.rs); [Cargo.lock:2505](../../../../Cargo.lock).

**Прямой прочитанный фрагмент:** [crates/shamir-connect/src/common/username.rs:8](../../../../crates/shamir-connect/src/common/username.rs): <code>//! 2. **Case Mapping** — Unicode case-fold (per RFC 8265, NOT raw / //!    `String::to_lowercase`; e.g. Turkish `İ` casefolds to `i\u{307}`, / //!    German `ß` to `ss`).</code>.

**Source rows / исходная risk-метка:** [shamir-connect/SUMMARY.md · Observation NEW.6](shamir-connect/SUMMARY.md#observation-new-6) (`nit`, accepted `confirmed-open`).

<a id="l4-rv-db-7c59e01b"></a>

### L4-RV-DB-7C59E01B — Nit: intentionally-leaked per-key lock maps are the only unbounded-growth sites — documented, but key count is unbounded by unique-name volume

**Дополнительный verdict:** `confirmed-open`. **Модули:** `shamir-db`. **Accepted canon:** [RV-DB-7C59E01B](P2.md#rv-db-7c59e01b) (allocation P2, scoped risk `nit`, verdict `confirmed-open`).

**Механизм / обязанность / граница:** Retention is explicitly documented and currently admin/schema-frequency. Catalogues and registries also grow, so these are not the only unbounded sites. Naive eviction can create two independent locks for one live key.

**Current source evidence:** [crates/shamir-db/src/shamir_db/shamir_db/core.rs:55](../../../../crates/shamir-db/src/shamir_db/shamir_db/core.rs); [crates/shamir-db/src/shamir_db/execute/admin_users_roles.rs:137](../../../../crates/shamir-db/src/shamir_db/execute/admin_users_roles.rs); [crates/shamir-db/src/shamir_db/shamir_db/core.rs:66](../../../../crates/shamir-db/src/shamir_db/shamir_db/core.rs); [crates/shamir-db/src/shamir_db/execute/admin_schema.rs:93](../../../../crates/shamir-db/src/shamir_db/execute/admin_schema.rs).

**Прямой прочитанный фрагмент:** [crates/shamir-db/src/shamir_db/shamir_db/core.rs:55](../../../../crates/shamir-db/src/shamir_db/shamir_db/core.rs): <code>/// admin commands target the same user.  Entries leak by design / /// (each unique user occupies a slot forever), but admin ops are / /// rare so the memory cost is negligible.</code>.

**Source rows / исходная risk-метка:** [shamir-db/SUMMARY.md · Claim 4.9](shamir-db/SUMMARY.md#review-4-9) (`nit`, accepted `confirmed-open`); [shamir-db/performance-hotpath.md · Claim 9](shamir-db/performance-hotpath.md#review-9) (`nit`, accepted `confirmed-open`).

<a id="l4-rv-funclib-50cd6b2e"></a>

### L4-RV-FUNCLIB-50CD6B2E — `ScalarRegistry::register` collision policy undocumented

**Дополнительный verdict:** `confirmed-open`. **Модули:** `shamir-funclib`. **Accepted canon:** [RV-FUNCLIB-50CD6B2E](P2.md#rv-funclib-50cd6b2e) (allocation P2, scoped risk `nit`, verdict `confirmed-open`).

**Механизм / обязанность / граница:** Scalar registration overwrites but does not document duplicate policy, unlike aggregate registration. Its container is Fx-backed std::HashMap, not the report's IndexMap. A duplicate-call oracle would pin last-wins; logging is optional.

**Counter-scope / оставшаяся premise:** Fx std::HashMap insertion overwrites but scalar docs omit last-wins, unlike aggregate docs.

**Current source evidence:** [crates/shamir-funclib/src/registry.rs:135](../../../../crates/shamir-funclib/src/registry.rs); [crates/shamir-funclib/src/agg.rs:72](../../../../crates/shamir-funclib/src/agg.rs); [crates/shamir-collections/src/lib.rs:43](../../../../crates/shamir-collections/src/lib.rs); [crates/shamir-funclib/src/registry.rs:126](../../../../crates/shamir-funclib/src/registry.rs).

**Source rows / исходная risk-метка:** [shamir-funclib/SUMMARY.md · Claim 5.9](shamir-funclib/SUMMARY.md#review-5-9) (`nit`, accepted `confirmed-open`); [shamir-funclib/api-wire-protocol.md · Claim 9](shamir-funclib/api-wire-protocol.md#review-9) (`nit`, accepted `confirmed-open`).

### L4-RV-INDEX-858BB5E1 — `ReaderDrainGate` doc invariant ("Never acquire any other lock while holding a `ReadGuard`") is contradicted in letter by `lookup_by_index`'s DashMap access inside the guard scope

**Дополнительный verdict:** `confirmed-open`. **Модули:** `shamir-index`. **Accepted canon:** [RV-INDEX-858BB5E1](P2.md#rv-index-858bb5e1) (allocation P2, scoped risk `nit`, verdict `confirmed-open`).

**Механизм / обязанность / граница:** Cache access acquires shard locks while the read guard lives. Observed cache guards release before awaits and before destructive operations; this shows wording drift, not a lock-order cycle.

**Counter-scope / оставшаяся premise:** DashMap cache accesses occur under ReadGuard; observed guards are leaf-scoped, not a demonstrated deadlock.

### L4-RV-NUMA-6E1761A2-FACET — Unsupported numerical bound in mirror-window documentation

**Дополнительный verdict:** `confirmed-open`. **Модули:** `shamir-numa`. **Accepted canon:** [RV-NUMA-6E1761A2](P0.md#rv-numa-6e1761a2) (allocation P0, scoped risk `high`, verdict `confirmed-open`).

<a id="l4-rv-transport-ipc-400d803e"></a>

### L4-RV-TRANSPORT-IPC-400D803E — Bind-collision error text is OS-asymmetric and unmapped

**Дополнительный verdict:** `confirmed-open`. **Модули:** `shamir-transport-ipc`. **Accepted canon:** [RV-TRANSPORT-IPC-400D803E](P2.md#rv-transport-ipc-400d803e) (allocation P2, scoped risk `nit`, verdict `confirmed-open`).

**Механизм / обязанность / граница:** Raw Unix bind errors and Windows first-instance creation errors remain unmapped. Tokio 1.49.0 published first_pipe_instance documentation identifies PermissionDenied for competing creation. This supports a diagnostic note, not unconditional conversion of every AccessDenied into a collision.

### L4-RV-TRANSPORT-TCP-5CE7BA5B — e2e tests run Argon2id inline on the test runtime thread while a peer task is spawned (fragile, no live failure)

**Дополнительный verdict:** `confirmed-open`. **Модули:** `shamir-transport-tcp`. **Accepted canon:** [RV-TRANSPORT-TCP-5CE7BA5B](P2.md#rv-transport-tcp-5ce7ba5b) (allocation P2, scoped risk `nit`, verdict `confirmed-open`).

**Механизм / обязанность / граница:** The synchronous derive occupies the current-thread worker while the server waits for proof. Adding a timeout does not necessarily produce a deadlock; its deadline and scheduling determine behavior.

**Counter-scope / оставшаяся premise:** Default current-thread tests call synchronous process_challenge. The peer is awaiting the proof, so this establishes worker occupancy, not a current deadlock or future timeout inevitability.

<a id="l4-rv-transport-tcp-7dfc88ff"></a>

### L4-RV-TRANSPORT-TCP-7DFC88FF — `Box<dyn Error + Send + Sync>` in library APIs instead of a `thiserror` enum

**Дополнительный verdict:** `confirmed-open`. **Модули:** `shamir-transport-tcp`. **Accepted canon:** [RV-TRANSPORT-TCP-7DFC88FF](P0.md#rv-transport-tcp-7dfc88ff) (allocation P0, scoped risk `nit`, verdict `confirmed-open`).

**Механизм / обязанность / граница:** Both TLS constructors retain boxed errors and a string missing-key case. Concrete causes can be downcast; server stringification is its own choice. An enumerable typed surface improves diagnostics/convention conformance, but boxing at this boundary is not a medium runtime defect.

**Counter-scope / оставшаяся premise:** Typed enumeration is absent, but concrete errors can be downcast and callers need not string-match. Typed variants improve contract assertions, but boxing does not prevent programmatic cause inspection. Downcasting remains possible; typed variants are a diagnostic/API improvement rather than demonstrated medium runtime harm. They are diagnostic/style limitations, not a demonstrated crypto failure, and concrete errors permit downcasting.

**Current source evidence:** [crates/shamir-transport-tcp/src/tls.rs:30](../../../../crates/shamir-transport-tcp/src/tls.rs); [crates/shamir-transport-tcp/src/tls.rs:44](../../../../crates/shamir-transport-tcp/src/tls.rs); [crates/shamir-transport-tcp/src/tls.rs:50](../../../../crates/shamir-transport-tcp/src/tls.rs); [crates/shamir-server/src/tls.rs:116](../../../../crates/shamir-server/src/tls.rs).

<a id="l4-rv-transport-ws-0b4767de"></a>

### L4-RV-TRANSPORT-WS-0B4767DE — Zero-length frame means "graceful close" on TCP but is a legal empty frame on WS — undocumented divergence in a claimed-identical wire format

**Дополнительный verdict:** `confirmed-open`. **Модули:** `shamir-transport-ws`. **Accepted canon:** [RV-TRANSPORT-WS-0B4767DE](P1.md#rv-transport-ws-0b4767de) (allocation P1, scoped risk `nit`, verdict `confirmed-open`).

**Механизм / обязанность / граница:** Four zero bytes succeed with an empty WS payload; TCP returns PeerClose. The shared byte layout remains correct and WS has its own Close control message. Clarification is warranted, but rejecting zero does not make behavior identical to TCP's graceful-close interpretation.

**Counter-scope / оставшаяся premise:** The distinction is real but shared byte layout does not imply identical transport-close semantics.

**Current source evidence:** [crates/shamir-transport-tcp/src/framing.rs:107](../../../../crates/shamir-transport-tcp/src/framing.rs); [crates/shamir-transport-ws/src/framing.rs:171](../../../../crates/shamir-transport-ws/src/framing.rs); [crates/shamir-transport-ws/src/framing.rs:173](../../../../crates/shamir-transport-ws/src/framing.rs); [crates/shamir-transport-ws/src/framing.rs:157](../../../../crates/shamir-transport-ws/src/framing.rs).

**Прямой прочитанный фрагмент:** [crates/shamir-transport-tcp/src/framing.rs:107](../../../../crates/shamir-transport-tcp/src/framing.rs): <code>if len == 0 { / return Err(FrameError::PeerClose); / }</code>.

**Source rows / исходная risk-метка:** [shamir-transport-ws/SUMMARY.md · Claim 5.5](shamir-transport-ws/SUMMARY.md#review-5-5) (`nit`, accepted `confirmed-open`); [shamir-transport-ws/api-wire-protocol.md · Claim 5](shamir-transport-ws/api-wire-protocol.md#review-5) (`nit`, accepted `confirmed-open`).

### L4-RV-TRANSPORT-WS-E6697C98 — Doc drift: `server_ws_config` claims a "4 KiB pre-auth logical check … enforced in `crate::framing::ws_recv_into`" — no such enforcement exists in this crate

**Дополнительный verdict:** `confirmed-open`. **Модули:** `shamir-transport-ws`. **Accepted canon:** [RV-TRANSPORT-WS-E6697C98](P2.md#rv-transport-ws-e6697c98) (allocation P2, scoped risk `nit`, verdict `confirmed-open`).

**Механизм / обязанность / граница:** Transport validation is phase-agnostic. The server chooses MAX_PRE_AUTH_FRAME after tungstenite has assembled the message; the documentation confuses caller policy with codec buffering.

**Counter-scope / оставшаяся premise:** The server selects the pre-auth cap; the transport validates only the supplied number after message assembly. It enforces the caller's cap, but authentication phase and 4 KiB selection live outside the adapter.

**Current source evidence:** [crates/shamir-transport-ws/src/server.rs:27](../../../../crates/shamir-transport-ws/src/server.rs); [crates/shamir-server/src/connection/handshake.rs:717](../../../../crates/shamir-server/src/connection/handshake.rs); [crates/shamir-transport-ws/src/framing.rs:151](../../../../crates/shamir-transport-ws/src/framing.rs); [crates/shamir-transport-ws/src/framing.rs:163](../../../../crates/shamir-transport-ws/src/framing.rs).

**Прямой прочитанный фрагмент:** [crates/shamir-transport-ws/src/server.rs:27](../../../../crates/shamir-transport-ws/src/server.rs): <code>/// pre-auth logical check (HIGH-1, enforced in &#91;`crate::framing::ws_recv_into`&#93;) / /// ever runs — 10 000 such peers ≈ 640 GiB. We pin both ceilings to the / /// spec §8 `MAX_FRAME_SIZE_DATA` value (16 MiB) so the per-connection</code>.

<a id="l4-rv-tunables-363b3879"></a>

### L4-RV-TUNABLES-363B3879 — No test pins cross-thread visibility of an override; `reads_are_shared_ref` runs single-threaded

**Дополнительный verdict:** `confirmed-open`. **Модули:** `shamir-tunables`. **Accepted canon:** [RV-TUNABLES-363B3879](P1.md#rv-tunables-363b3879) (allocation P1, scoped risk `nit`, verdict `confirmed-open`).

**Механизм / обязанность / граница:** Arc cloning and both accesses occur on one thread. A writer joined before reading can guard synchronized sharing and Send + Sync compatibility; it cannot prove immediate cross-thread freshness without synchronization.

**Counter-scope / оставшаяся premise:** A joined writer would pin synchronized sharing, but would not prove immediate unsynchronized freshness or distinguish Relaxed from stronger ordering.

**Current source evidence:** [crates/shamir-tunables/src/tests/runtime_tests.rs:52](../../../../crates/shamir-tunables/src/tests/runtime_tests.rs); [crates/shamir-tunables/src/runtime.rs:18](../../../../crates/shamir-tunables/src/runtime.rs).

### L4-RV-WASM-HOST-8062B59A — Dead `Arc` clones on every `batch_get` / `global_get` host import

**Дополнительный verdict:** `confirmed-open`. **Модули:** `shamir-wasm-host`. **Accepted canon:** [RV-WASM-HOST-8062B59A](P2.md#rv-wasm-host-8062b59a) (allocation P2, scoped risk `nit`, verdict `confirmed-open`).

**Механизм / обязанность / граница:** Successful batch/global reads clone two handles that only reach discard expressions. Absent/denied reads return earlier. These are structural cleanup, not measured performance regressions.

**Counter-scope / оставшаяся premise:** Successful getter paths clone handles consumed only by discard expressions; absent/denied paths avoid them.

<a id="l4-rv-wasm-host-ff7c2d6e"></a>

### L4-RV-WASM-HOST-FF7C2D6E — WASM maintenance bundle: duplicate prose/glob code and fallback descriptions

**Дополнительный verdict:** `confirmed-open`. **Модули:** `shamir-wasm-host`. **Accepted canon:** [RV-WASM-HOST-FF7C2D6E](P2.md#rv-wasm-host-ff7c2d6e) (allocation P2, scoped risk `nit`, verdict `confirmed-open`).

**Механизм / обязанность / граница:** The old composite names duplicate host-call prose and glob implementations, retained ticker lifetime, empty-path fallbacks and malformed-grant filtering. This nit slice is maintenance presentation/duplication, not a certificate for those runtime policies.

**Counter-scope / оставшаяся premise:** Actual repeated-suffix glob rejection is the separate low observation [L3-RV-WASM-HOST-935F15D1](P3.md#l3-rv-wasm-host-935f15d1). Existing canonical ticker/path/grant counter-scopes remain in force; the bundle is not five independent security exploits and removing duplicate prose proves none of those behaviors.

## Отдельно: higher-risk mechanisms, не low/nit очередь

Эти original low/nit строки не потеряны: current accepted mechanisms остаются medium в P0–P2; они не перечислены как активные P4 defects. Scope/условия и ссылки ниже позволяют отличить новое qualification от fixed/refuted.

<a id="l4-rv-index-c3fbd7ac-redirect"></a>

### L4-RV-INDEX-C3FBD7AC-REDIRECT — AArch64 safe SQ8 byte-slice callers can reach an alignment-requiring u32 lane load

**Дополнительный verdict:** `higher-risk-only`. **Модули:** `shamir-index`. **Accepted canon:** [RV-INDEX-C3FBD7AC](P2.md#rv-index-c3fbd7ac) (allocation P2, scoped risk `medium`, verdict `confirmed-open`).

**Механизм / обязанность / граница:** The exact Rust 1.95 intrinsic typed *ptr load resolves the old alignment premise. Safe AArch64 SQ8 byte-slice offsets of residue 1/2/3 mod4 and length >=4 can reach it; this is the accepted conditional medium soundness mechanism, not a nit. Original plan P2.21 stays P2. [Rust 1.95 vld1_lane_u32:16503–16506](https://doc.rust-lang.org/1.95.0/src/core/stdarch/crates/core_arch/src/arm_shared/neon/generated.rs.html#16503-16506) evaluates simd_insert!(src, LANE as u32, *ptr); [exact simd_insert macro](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/rust-lang/rust/1.95.0/library/stdarch/crates/core_arch/src/macros.rs) forwards the value expression. [Rust 1.95 misaligned load rule](https://doc.rust-lang.org/1.95.0/reference/behavior-considered-undefined.html#misaligned-pointers) applies to the typed dereference, not merely to a pointer cast or hardware LD1 restriction.

**Counter-scope / оставшаяся premise:** Собственный accepted verdict каждой строки сохранён в ledger; redirect означает исключение из дополнительной low/nit очереди, не fixed, refuted или safe. Bounds checks establish four bytes, not u32 alignment; no explicit read_unaligned/alignment exemption was found in that pinned intrinsic body. Owned vectors often align, while legal offset &[u8] slices need not. Other target/deployment/stack facets are not executed or certified.

**Current source evidence:** [crates/shamir-index/src/vector/simd.rs:932](../../../../crates/shamir-index/src/vector/simd.rs); [rust-toolchain.toml:15](../../../../rust-toolchain.toml); [crates/shamir-index/src/vector/simd.rs:920](../../../../crates/shamir-index/src/vector/simd.rs); [crates/shamir-index/src/vector/simd.rs:1101](../../../../crates/shamir-index/src/vector/simd.rs); [crates/shamir-index/src/vector/simd.rs:1250](../../../../crates/shamir-index/src/vector/simd.rs); [crates/shamir-index/src/vector/simd.rs:811](../../../../crates/shamir-index/src/vector/simd.rs); [crates/shamir-index/src/vector/sq8.rs:222](../../../../crates/shamir-index/src/vector/sq8.rs); [crates/shamir-index/src/vector/sq8.rs:295](../../../../crates/shamir-index/src/vector/sq8.rs); [crates/shamir-index/src/vector/sq8.rs:348](../../../../crates/shamir-index/src/vector/sq8.rs); [crates/shamir-index/src/vector/quantized_dist.rs:460](../../../../crates/shamir-index/src/vector/quantized_dist.rs); [crates/shamir-index/src/vector/tests/simd_tests.rs:371](../../../../crates/shamir-index/src/vector/tests/simd_tests.rs); [crates/shamir-index/src/vector/tests/mod.rs:1](../../../../crates/shamir-index/src/vector/tests/mod.rs).

**Прямой прочитанный фрагмент:** [crates/shamir-index/src/vector/simd.rs:932](../../../../crates/shamir-index/src/vector/simd.rs): <code>let xv_u8 = vld1_lane_u32(xp.add(i) as *const u32, vdup_n_u32(0), 0); / let yv_u8 = vld1_lane_u32(yp.add(i) as *const u32, vdup_n_u32(0), 0); / let xv_u8x8 = vreinterpret_u8_u32(xv_u8);</code>.

**Source rows / исходная risk-метка:** [shamir-index/SUMMARY.md · Claim 3.7](shamir-index/SUMMARY.md#review-3-7) (`nit`, accepted `confirmed-open`); [shamir-index/security-crypto.md · Claim 7](shamir-index/security-crypto.md#review-7) (`nit`, accepted `confirmed-open`).

<a id="l4-rv-transport-ws-624f81b1-redirect"></a>

### L4-RV-TRANSPORT-WS-624F81B1-REDIRECT — Send path has no frame-size cap and truncates the length prefix at `u32::MAX` — diverges from the TCP sibling

**Дополнительный verdict:** `higher-risk-only`. **Модули:** `shamir-transport-ws`. **Accepted canon:** [RV-TRANSPORT-WS-624F81B1](P1.md#rv-transport-ws-624f81b1) (allocation P1, scoped risk `medium`, verdict `confirmed-open`).

**Механизм / обязанность / граница:** Missing local ceiling and unchecked usize→u32 narrowing are the accepted medium send-boundary mechanism. A >=2^32 caller-owned payload is conditional and huge, but the mechanism is not pure nit/style and is not reassigned here.

**Counter-scope / оставшаяся premise:** Собственный accepted verdict каждой строки сохранён в ledger; redirect означает исключение из дополнительной low/nit очереди, не fixed, refuted или safe.

**Current source evidence:** [crates/shamir-transport-ws/src/framing.rs:118](../../../../crates/shamir-transport-ws/src/framing.rs); [crates/shamir-server/src/config.rs:418](../../../../crates/shamir-server/src/config.rs); [crates/shamir-transport-tcp/src/framing.rs:153](../../../../crates/shamir-transport-tcp/src/framing.rs); [Cargo.lock:4467](../../../../Cargo.lock); [crates/shamir-transport-ws/src/framing.rs:157](../../../../crates/shamir-transport-ws/src/framing.rs).

**Прямой прочитанный фрагмент:** [crates/shamir-transport-ws/src/framing.rs:118](../../../../crates/shamir-transport-ws/src/framing.rs): <code>let len = payload.len() as u32; / let mut buf = Vec::with_capacity(4 + payload.len()); / buf.extend_from_slice(&amp;len.to_be_bytes());</code>.

## Inventory and arithmetic provenance

[Все 189 inputs и per-document low/nit/primary counts](P3.md#supplemental-input-inventory); [оригинальный full inventory 3176 decisions](P0.md#input-inventory). Это shared inventory, не исключение thematic reports/observations из этого среза.

## Static-only evidence limits и сохранность

Проверяются только готовые Markdown: actual primary-key uniqueness/coverage, arithmetic, local target/anchors, cited file:line bounds, readback/fingerprints и сохранность inputs/current source/старых P0–P2. External URLs — direct provenance, не запуск dependency/runtime. Fixed не означает tests passed; unverified/high-risk-only не означает safe. Историческая mixed style delivery не исправлена этим review. Блоков реализации, placeholders и требований выполнить проект в этом продолжении нет.
